stopped_at: "THREE OPERATOR ITEMS SHIPPED 2026-08-29 (7350ce2e, b6cfc7b7, 306e47c5). ONE THING OWED AND IT IS A LOOK, NOT A FIX: the `cmd` palette's rendered rows could not be screenshotted — the launcher dismisses the instant the pointer leaves it (follow_mouse + HyprlandFocusGrab) and there is no input-injection path here, so load and activation are verified and LAYOUT IS NOT. Type `cmd`, check the two-column row (leaf name left, breadcrumb right), then `cmd theme` + Enter. Also judge the horizontal Continuous bar in daily use — measured at both ends and mid-screen but only flipped to and back. Note the composition it ACTUALLY produces: 4px rim above the bar, and a fused 10px gradient edge BELOW it, because the strip lands under a horizontal bar rather than over it. (1) `cmd` is a SEPARATE file from MenuMode, not a mode switch inside it — the two disagree on both of MenuMode's load-bearing properties (navStack, and `_stickyModes` membership, which exists precisely so a keystroke cannot re-route the surface). (2) THE GLYPH INSET IS ARITHMETIC: the core is 44 wide, its cap centred y=32 r=22, and barContent was inset by _weldCapDepth alone — so a 44-WIDE capsule box started at the cap's APEX, corners outside the shape. A 44-wide box first fits in a 44-wide pill at the TANGENT, 10+22=32; measured after at glyph centre 43 vs predicted 44. (3) THE PLAN'S ASSUMPTION WAS WRONG: baredge-top sits at y=48 with a horizontal bar up, BELOW it, because a non-negative exclusive zone is positioned inside every existing zone. Reserving the whole slab lands the strip flush and the shared gradient mapping makes the seam invisible. DURABLE TRAP, NEW INSTANCE: every gate passed through a live ReferenceError — a lazily-loaded component is a gate blind spot BY CONSTRUCTION, and only opening the surface and reading the log saw it. SECOND, CHEAPER RECOVERY FOR THE INODE TRAP: an in-place append to a file whose watch is still alive (shell.qml) forces a whole-config reload and re-reads the orphaned file — no unit restart, no cgroup argument. STILL OWED FROM THE PREVIOUS SESSION: judge `precise` (the desktop is on it), and the four 1px dividers moved outline -> outlineVariant. NEXT REAL DECISION unchanged: v5.0 has no roadmap — /gsd-review-backlog then /gsd-new-milestone."

## Accumulated Context

### Roadmap Evolution

- Phase 9 added: wlogout to wleave Migration (GTK4) — decided 2026-07-13 after Phase 6's wlogout redesign; driver is GTK3's whole-stylesheet-discard failure class (WLOG-01), NOT the blur limitation, which is compositor-global and unfixable by any layer-shell client.
- v3.0 roadmapped 2026-07-26: Phases 11-17, continuing numbering from v2.0's Phase 10. 38 requirements mapped 1:1 to phases, no orphans, no duplicates.
  - **11 Quickshell Viability Gate** (QS-01..06, MAINT-01) — STOP authority; also runs the workspace-overview screencopy feasibility probe early, per ARCHITECTURE Correction 2.
  - **12 Unified Design-Token Pipeline** (TOKEN-01..06) — colour + MD3 motion to QML/GTK4 CSS/Hyprland; TOKEN-06 (springs) is a stretch that blocks nothing.
  - **13 Motion Retrofit & Existing-Surface Sweep** (MOTION-01..03, MAINT-02, MAINT-03) and **14 Dashboard Drawer** (DASH-01..08) are **independent branches, both gated only on 12** — not serially dependent on each other (ARCHITECTURE Correction 1). 13 runs first as risk reduction, not as a dependency.
  - **15 Audio + Connectivity Panels** (PANEL-01..06) — depends on 14; carries the milestone's highest D-Bus conflict risk.
  - **16 Workspace Overview** (OVER-01..04) — protocol question is settled (`ScreencopyView` + `hyprland-toplevel-export-v1`, no plugin); the real open risk is live multi-window screencopy *performance*, which OVER-04 makes a measured requirement with a documented fallback.
  - **17 Ambient Extras** (AMB-01..02) — explicitly the first phase to cut.
  - MAINT placement rationale and coverage proof live in REQUIREMENTS.md's Traceability section.
- v4.0 roadmapped 2026-08-10: Phases 18-22, continuing numbering from v3.0's Phase 17 (which included an inserted 13.1). 55 requirements mapped 1:1 to phases, no orphans, no duplicates. Granularity `coarse`.
  - **18 QML Bar & Retirement Machinery** (QBAR-01..12, RETIRE-01/02, GATE-01..04, LEDGER-01/03) — first, per the research's dependency analysis and PROJECT.md's own framing: highest daily contact, and its patterns (always-on `PanelWindow`, `exclusiveZone > 0`, config-driven entry list, restart wrapper, retirement checklist script) seed every later surface. First surface with no dismissed state, so it inherits none of the zero-idle discipline; first with `exclusiveZone > 0`; shares a process with Phase 19's notification server. Must NOT attempt per-screen fan-out (QS-03 permanently dropped under D-13) — copy `Overview.qml`'s single-`PanelWindow` pattern.
  - **19 Notification Server & Centre** (QNOTIF-01..11, RETIRE-03, LEDGER-04/07/08) — after the bar (the centre button lives on it). swaync is **deleted in the same phase, no soak window** (user's call, against the research's disabled-but-installed recommendation), so there is no rollback: QNOTIF-05 (`replaces_id`) and QNOTIF-11 (live two-owner check, not self-test) carry extra verification weight. The autostart swap must be one atomic edit.
  - **20 Indicators & Power Menu** (QOSD-01..04, QPOWER-01..04, RETIRE-04/05/07, LEDGER-02/05) — OSD reuses the transient-toast frame built in 19; the power menu is independently schedulable (no shared backend, lowest risk) and may be built in parallel inside this phase rather than occupying a thin phase of its own. `swayosd-libinput-backend.service`'s fate is a named scope call needing explicit sign-off, BAR-02-style.
  - **21 Media Fold-In & Contract Close** (QMEDIA-01..03, RETIRE-06/08, LEDGER-06) — last of the migrations; **opens with a blocking cava go/no-go spike** (QMEDIA-02) before any design work. Note waybar's mpris reader already died in 18, so this removes the second of three readers, not the third.
  - **22 Fresh-Install Proof** (RETIRE-09) — the milestone's closing regression gate, deliberately a single-requirement phase: it structurally cannot run until all five deletions land, and running it earlier would only prove the pre-migration state. Carries no debt.
  - **GATE-01 is per-phase, not an upfront phase** — the enumeration must be read off the live implementation while it still exists, and nothing is deleted before its own phase. GATE-02 is a per-phase closing gate. Both are mapped to 18 as their establishing phase. Full justification in REQUIREMENTS.md § Traceability → Placement notes.
  - **Debt is interleaved across 18-21, never parked** — research explicitly warns debt-paydown dies silently when migration runs long, and v3.0's closeout names this as the open test of v4.0. If the milestone runs long, cut migration stretch goals first, never the debt.
- Phase 10 added: AGS Media Applet — decided 2026-07-15 after the Phase 08 eww media popup was proven unable to deliver pointer input to its widgets on this eww 0.6.0 / Hyprland 0.55.4 build (debug session resolved-by-redesign: `.planning/debug/resolved/eww-media-popup-clicks-dead.md`). Replace with a standalone AGS v3 (GTK4) media applet, keeping waybar/swaync/matugen. User rejected a full HyprPanel bar takeover in favor of a contained applet. Approved spec + plan live in `docs/superpowers/`.
- Phase 13.1 inserted after Phase 13: Hyprland Lua Config Migration — 0.57 removes .conf support; hyprlang replaced by Lua. Inserted before Dashboard Drawer because phases 14-17 all consume the design tokens this rewrites. (URGENT)
- Phase 18.1 inserted after Phase 18: QML Bar Athena Restoration — operator judged the Phase 18 QML bar worse than the Athena waybar it replaces (GATE-02 verdict). Restores Athena's visual language: BarRoles colour layer, filled pills, hover-reveal drawers, Nerd Font workspace glyphs with pacman state set, icon-only resource pills, tray dropped. Design: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-11-qml-bar-athena-restoration-design.md (ca54fb1). Must complete before 18-19 (GATE-02) and 18-20 (waybar retirement). (URGENT)

- [Phase ?]: QS-02 PASSED on first attempt under WlrKeyboardFocus.OnDemand — no Exclusive escalation needed; Phase 14's drawer inherits OnDemand as the default
- [Phase ?]: keybind-doctor repaired: dropped hyprctl binds -j (field-misaligned on Hyprland 0.56.0, not malformed) for plain-text parsing with a named shape guard; extended to cross-check Quickshell GlobalShortcut manifest against hyprctl globalshortcuts (D-16/D-17); D-18 poisoned-fixture proof confirmed the collision check fires; ROADMAP/REQUIREMENTS wording amended per D-15
- [Phase ?]: quickshell-doctor established: before/during/after diff for hyprctl monitors -j reserved array (never a hardcoded literal); trap-armed-before-mutation restore pattern for volume/brightness probes; QS-05/QS-06 both PASS
- [Phase ?]: QS-03 per-screen mounting fix (Variants-based) attempted and reverted after two independent live-daemon reliability regressions; recorded as an open, non-blocking gap per D-10
- [Phase ?]: QS-04 open question #1 answered affirmatively: FileView/JsonAdapter needs zero reload.sh involvement; lib/reload.sh left untouched (D-13 negative branch)
- [Phase ?]: Phase 11 verdict: PASS. QS-02 (sole stop-trigger) passed; criterion 5 screencopy feasibility confirmed (human-attested multi-window capture); permissions.conf ships mechanism-verified but inert, live enforcement deferred to Phase 16 OVER-04
- [Phase ?]: Second Quickshell GlobalShortcut costs one manifest entry + one keybind line (D-17 confirmed), but requires a process restart to register — QML hot-reload alone is insufficient for new GlobalShortcut registration
- [Phase ?]: HyprlandFocusGrab is exclusive per-compositor on this build: two Quickshell surfaces cannot be shown simultaneously; disclosed, non-blocking, relevant to Phases 14/16

QS-03 left open, un-marked-complete; D-13 permanent-limitation decision deferred to Plan 12-02's opening checkpoint.
- [Phase ?]: quickshell-doctor's one-step-per-press volume-probe gate is over-strict exact-match on rounding-sensitive raw units (pre-existing, Phase 11, unrelated to 12-01) — filed as pending todo quickshell-doctor-volume-probe-brittle, not fixed
- [Phase ?]: 12-03: motion.json's floor_ms lives as data, not a shell constant, so the multiplier table and floor retune together as a data edit
- [Phase ?]: 12-03: engine_owned_files array now drives BOTH commit.sh's rsync excludes and theme-doctor's new D-29 state-manifest gate — closed a bug class that had drifted 8 times; ags.scss (Phase 10 AGS applet) was a pre-existing contract.json gap closed alongside it with a new scss-vars format
- [Phase ?]: 12-02: D-13 resolved accept-permanent — QS-03 (per-screen Quickshell fan-out) dropped to Out of Scope in REQUIREMENTS.md/PROJECT.md/ROADMAP.md; quickshell-doctor's per-screen check converted to a documented D-14 SKIP (QS03_ACCEPTED_PERMANENT guard), reversibility verified live
- [Phase ?]: 12-02: quickshell-doctor still exits 1 (12 passed, 1 failed) — the remaining FAIL is a pre-existing, unrelated volume-probe gate brittleness (filed in 12-01), left unfixed per scope discipline
- [Phase ?]: 12-04: motion-scale CLI + GSettings enable-animations wired; fixed jq `// empty` bug in motion.sh that silently broke the 'off' preset (JSON false treated as absent)
- [Phase ?]: 12-04: hyprland.conf sources hyprland-motion.conf before config/animations.conf (D-22); stow.sh seeds motion-scale + rendered motion files by invoking motion.sh's renderer, never a stub (D-30)
- [Phase ?]: 12-04: theme-doctor's hyprctl readback and theme-parity's D-31 byte-identity assertion both proven to fail before being trusted to pass; Hyprland silently DROPS a malformed bezier curve rather than clamping/erroring — the concrete observed failure mode
- [Phase ?]: 12-05: motion-lint (waybar-design-lint shape + keybind-doctor path-argument precedent) folded into theme-doctor — CHECK A reference resolution, CHECK B raw-value refusal, CHECK C scanned-count floor, 7-entry exemption list, 9 committed compliant/poisoned fixtures with a --self-test mode
- [Phase ?]: 12-05: fixed 3 Rule-1 bugs found live — os.walk() default not following stow's symlinked subdirectories silently dropped surfaces; QML definition set omitted motionEnabled (Pitfall 4); the default scan picked up the lint's own fixtures directory nested inside the hypr config tree
- [Phase ?]: 12-06: Colours.qml/Motion.qml need BOTH pragma Singleton and qmldir's singleton keyword to resolve bare TypeName.property access — corrects 12-RESEARCH.md Pattern 2, binary-verified
- [Phase ?]: 12-06: a pragma-Singleton QML type cannot declare property X alongside property onX in the same object (Material You's role/onRole convention) — Qt's AOT singleton compiler misparses onX as a signal handler; fixed in Colours.qml via two sibling FileView/JsonAdapter pairs (base/on-roles)

| 260829-2ov | **`cmd` COMMAND PALETTE, THE WELD'S END GLYPHS, AND THE HORIZONTAL WELD.** (1) `cmd` flattens MenuTree's 35 leaves into one fuzzy list — a SEPARATE file from MenuMode, not a mode switch inside it, because the two disagree on both of MenuMode's load-bearing properties: `menu` walks navStack and sits in `_stickyModes` precisely so a keystroke CANNOT re-route the surface, while this ignores navStack and MUST fall back to apps mode when the query stops starting with "cmd". Activation branches copied in MenuMode's order rather than refactored into a shared helper — re-homing them in the commit that introduces a new surface would make a regression here indistinguishable from one there. (2) GLYPH INSET IS DERIVED, NOT TUNED. Measured first: the core is 44px wide (barColumnWidth exactly) with its pill cap centred at y=32 radius 22, core top y=10 — and barContent was inset by `_weldCapDepth` alone, so the first capsule's 44-WIDE box began AT THE CAP'S APEX where the pill has zero width, corners outside the shape, app glyph at y16-27. A 44-wide box first fits inside a 44-wide pill at the CAP TANGENT: 10+22=32. Re-measured after: glyph centre 43 vs predicted 44, every other capsule moved by exactly the same 22. (3) THE PLAN'S OWN ASSUMPTION WAS WRONG AND MEASURING CAUGHT IT — `hyprctl layers` puts baredge-top at y=48 with a horizontal bar up, BELOW the bar, not above it: a non-negative exclusive zone is positioned inside every existing zone. So the "top rail" is the bar's UNDERSIDE rail there, and the operator's chosen sketch does not describe the actual stack. Reserving the bar's body alone made the strip's 6px run OVERLAP the slab's bottom rim and poke past its cap at the ends; reserving the whole slab lands the strip flush at 56, and because both paint through the SAME `_stripGradX1`/`_stripPeriod` mapping the seam is invisible (measured x=1280: rim 203,141,238 then rail 203,141,237). `_continuousWeld` KEEPS its exact old meaning rather than being widened — it gates six things wrong on a horizontal bar (margin collapse, implicitWidth growth, both stubs, the vertical slab, popoutBulgeDepth, two PopoutController bindings) and widening it would have switched all six on silently. DURABLE FINDING: EVERY GATE PASSED THROUGH A LIVE ReferenceError. CommandMode shipped without `import "../dashboard"` so `Design` was undefined, and colour-lint 575/0, qml-import-check 0-unresolved/193, singleton-prop-check 0 and a clean hot-reload line ALL agreed the file was fine while the surface threw 18 errors per open — the name parses and only fails when the binding is EVALUATED, inside a component the LazyLoader never instantiates until the mode is entered. A lazily-loaded component is a gate blind spot BY CONSTRUCTION; the only instrument was opening it and reading the log. SECOND: the inode trap's recovery is cheaper than recorded — an in-place append to a file whose watch is STILL ALIVE (shell.qml) forces a whole-config reload and re-reads the orphaned file, so no unit restart and no cgroup safety argument is needed. Verified live in BOTH orientations and restored byte-identical (vertical reserved [0,6,50,6], bar surface 2478,0 76x1440). Gates: quickshell-doctor 28/0, colour-lint 575/0, motion-lint 814/0, transparent-lint 194/0, settings-index-check 191/0, qml-import-check 0/193, singleton-prop-check 0.  ROUND 2 (operator feedback, fb0ab0b8 + 50d93b5f): (a) THE DESIGN IS A THREE-SIDED FRAME, and stating the rule that way made the rest fall out — Continuous draws THREE runs, the bar is one of them, the fourth side is open; vertical = top+bottom rails with the bar on the right, horizontal = right+bottom rails with the bar on the TOP. That makes mounting the right rail and UNmounting the top strip one decision, not two. Round 1's 'keep the top strip' was wrong ON MEASUREMENT: with a horizontal bar it lands at y=56, UNDER the bar, a second parallel band — and at the open left end the two capped differently (25px pill vs 3px), so the strip protruded ~25px past the slab's curve. The flare reuses Design.edgeBarWeldFlareRadius because its vertical derivation does NOT transfer (this band's end is square, not capped, so a quarter arc is tangent at any radius and the value is free) — reusing the token is exactly what makes the orientations match. SWEEP FLAG DERIVED NUMERICALLY BEFORE WRITING: _arcCentre gives (X,0) for sweepPositive and (X-F,F) against it, _shoulderSweep maps true->1, so flag 0. Cost stated up front: the top strip carried the dashboard's bulge and dwell-summon, so the dashboard spawns unattached there — not a new state, brackets has no bulge on any edge either. (b) ICON STRIP: clipping was arithmetic — a 608px box holding 1112px of tiles in a bare Row, which is a POSITIONER, so anchoring it left+right sets where it starts and never how wide its children may be, and with no clip tiles 5-8 painted over the desktop. Now a ListView, which also fixes what a Row never could: Left/Right past tile 4 walked the selection off the panel. THE 27.7s WAS NOT THE CACHE — _find_icon_at_size ran one find PER NAME PER TIER, up to 60 walks of a 305,764-file Papirus tree where one bare walk is 164ms; one traversal ranked by the same unchanged tier ladder gives 3,862ms, 7.2x, byte-identical on all 8 themes. Disk cache added, served instantly with one probe behind it so a dangling path self-corrects — no fingerprint, because a theme list or an index.theme mtime is right about some updates and wrong about others. (c) 'HORIZONTAL BAR ONLY APPEARS ON HOVER' IS NOT A REGRESSION — measured: at hidden-idle the slab IS painted (rim 6-10, core 10-52, rim 52-62) and only the glyphs hide, exactly as vertical has always done; it is bar.autoHideOnIdle, made conspicuous by a full-width frame. | 260829-prc | **SIXTH STYLE `PRECISE` — CONSTANT VELOCITY, THE ONLY SYMMETRIC IN/OUT.** Picked the way gravity was, by measuring the gap. TWO axes were empty. SHAPE: every other style is a curve that decelerates, which is exactly why `linear`/`css-linear` sat in the base easings table used by ZERO styles; precise covers 10% of the distance by t=10% against 32-62% for the others (gravity 1%). SYMMETRY: every other style is slow-in/fast-out at ratios 1.43-3.00; precise is exactly 1.00, in and out both 250ms, which nothing else does. Curve is [0.12,0.12,0.88,0.88] NOT a true linear [0,0,1,1] — a true linear starts and stops with infinite jerk and reads cheap, so this holds constant velocity across the middle and rounds only the ends. UNIFORMITY IS THE IDENTITY: every easing name is the same curve and every leaf the same speed, the statement being that no channel is treated more dramatically than another; the emphasized pair needed no thought because the 260829-wvy derivation returns the same curve with no overshoot to clamp. Its leaves carry NO `style` key, so they take Hyprland's plain default instead of base's popin 60% — undecorated, the point of the style. TOKEN BAND RENUMBERED while one commit old: appending micro5=70 and extra-long6=1100 left the bands out of MAGNITUDE order (45/60/80/100/70), a smell a future contributor would trip on; bands now ascend (micro 45/60/70/80/100, extra-long 700..1200) with bouncy/gravity repointed to their UNCHANGED VALUES under new names — no style's timing moved. All six styles render with zero configerrors and pass hypr-equivalence 3/0 INDIVIDUALLY, the first time every style has been checked against the baseline rather than just the active one. Gates: motion-lint 811/0, --self-test 12/12, quickshell-doctor 28/0, colour-lint 572/0, keybind-doctor 13/0. | 2026-08-29 | 15c07425 | [260829-prc](./quick/260829-prc-precise-style/) |
| 260829-rhy | **THREE UNEXPLOITED AXES OPENED — RHYTHM, AMBIENT, AND MD3/ZEN SHARPENED.** Found by asking, for every lever in motion.json, WHICH ONES HAS NO STYLE EVER TOUCHED — not by brainstorming. RHYTHM WAS THE BIG ONE: `stagger-offset` was 50ms in EVERY style and had never been varied, yet it is consumed by 11 files plus the shared Cascade primitive (whole lock screen, power menu, overview, dashboard panels, settings pages, screensaver). Every style animated each element with its own personality and then choreographed them all identically — the 7-band dashboard's last band started 300ms behind the first in all five. Now 45/50/60/80/100 for snappy/md3/zen/bouncy/gravity. NEEDED NEW TOKENS since M3's scale starts at short1=50, too coarse for five rhythms; micro1..4 extend BELOW short1, and 45 not 40 for the tightest because floor_ms IS 40 and everything is clamped up to it, so a 40ms token would be indistinguishable from the floor AND would stop scaling under the accessibility multiplier. Verified first that nothing in code or any gate hardcodes duration-token names. AMBIENT went 1000-everywhere to 700-1200, AND THAT EXPOSED A LATENT COUPLING: hypr-equivalence dropped 3/0 -> 2/1 on exactly one record, `leaf 14 (border) baseline speed=10.0 live=12.0`. Hyprland's border leaf read `ambient` directly in animations.lua — but ambient is a LOOP PERIOD for continuous QML motion while the border leaf is a ONE-SHOT focus-change transition; two different things sharing one token, harmless until ambient became per-style. DECOUPLED, NOT WAIVED: the border transition got its own `indicators` entry (styles cannot override indicators) and keeps the 1000ms the baseline pins; ambient is QML-only now, which is what it was always for. hypr-equivalence is 3/0 under EVERY style now, where before it was only ever verified under one. MD3/ZEN: distinct in character (md3 the only fully monotonic style at peak 1.000 vs zen's 1.092; md3 front-loads hardest at 62% by t=10% and zen least at 32%; zen the only multi-segment curve) BUT 260829-grv had given md3 smooth's durations and smooth happened to match zen on 4 of 8 channels, so half of zen's timing signature had stopped contributing. RESOLVED ON MD3'S SIDE, NOT ZEN'S — zen's durations are an exact port of Caelestia's tokens.hpp and the reference wins. All 10 channels differ now. TRAP REPEATED: a `_comment` STRING inside a table lib/motion.sh ITERATES breaks the renderer; the file already warns about this for `semantic` and it is equally true for `indicators`, where I hit it — the tell is an empty rendered motion.json and border speed 0.0. That is a speed dial wearing two names, exactly what 260821-swp abolished when it replaced the motion-scale multiplier with style-as-personality; the dial had grown back INSIDE the style axis. THE LEAVES CAME FREE AND THAT WAS WORTH CHECKING FIRST — I was about to write md3 a full hypr_leaves block; motion.sh says an absent `speed_key` means USE THE LEAF'S OWN CURVE NAME as the speed name, so windows_in reads spatial-in's new 400ms automatically and md3 still overrides zero leaves. NEW STYLE PICKED BY MEASURING THE EMPTY AXIS, NOT BY CHOOSING A MOOD: every pre-existing style front-loads, covering 30-62% of the distance in the first 10% of the time (md3 62, snappy 51, smooth 44, bouncy 41, zen 32). NOTHING IN THE SET STARTS GENTLY, so nothing has weight. `gravity` covers 0.8%. Its spatial-out genuinely accelerates away, which no other style does. TWO THINGS HAD TO BE MEASURED: the landing is a thud not a bounce (peak 1.0135, just under zen's 1.014, far below bouncy's 1.097 — mass settles, it does not spring); and A SLOW START DAMPS A TRAILING OVERSHOOT ENORMOUSLY, so the obvious first draft [0.75,0,0.25,1.10] peaked at only 1.0064 (invisible) and x2 had to move 0.25 -> 0.65 before the settle registered — found by searching the curve space against two constraints, not by eye. | 260826-oyu | FOUR OPERATOR-FOUND DEFECTS, none reachable by any gate — all four gates green throughout. (1) DRILLING INTO A SUB-PAGE BROKE KEYBOARD NAV, and it took two rounds because there were two causes stacked. Round 1: `_recollectRows()` ended with an unconditional `contentFocused = false`, so a push re-collected the sub-page's rows and then threw focus back to the rail. Real fix, still failed. Round 2 found the one underneath: `_collectFocusableRows` inferred 'not the current stack element' from `visible === false`, and THAT INFERENCE IS FALSE FOR THE WHOLE LENGTH OF A PUSH TRANSITION — StackPage pushes with StackView.PushTransition and QQC2 keeps BOTH items visible while it animates, so the next-tick re-collect also picked up the parent page's rows, first in declaration order. Focus was carried correctly and landed on row 0 of the page UNDERNEATH. Fixed by asking the StackView: `currentItem` is set SYNCHRONOUSLY inside push(), before any animation, so the timing question disappears rather than being tuned around with a longer delay that would work here and break on slower hardware. Two same-class holes closed with it: focusRowsInvalidated now KEEPS pane focus (it means 'my rows changed', not 'the user changed page'), and a focus request for a page with no rows yet is stashed until they arrive. (2) THE BROWSE DIALOG WAS INPUT-DEAD because HyprlandFocusGrab captures input EXCLUSIVELY to the surfaces it lists and the FilePicker is a separate FloatingWindow toplevel that was not one — clicks landed on the settings window behind AND read as clicks outside the grab, which is why it also dismissed. Registered through the shared SettingsState so the generic picker stays uncoupled. (3) LIVE TILES WERE ZOOMED AND CLIPPED. Captured the running carousel, which is its own control: 16:9 frame, 1920x1080 sources, so PreserveAspectCrop should be a no-op — poster neighbours whole, playing tile cut. Same geometry, same fillMode, ELEMENT TYPE the only variable. A hypothesis died on the way (GIF frames can legally be canvas sub-rects; QImageReader says 1920x1080 and all 150 frames full-size — not that), so the fix targets the surviving SIGNATURE, left-anchored overflow: cover-size the media box and CENTRE it instead of trusting the toolkit's crop. Worst case becomes a centred crop, never a corner. (4) LIVE WALLPAPERS SORTED TO THE END of the carousel (86-88 of 88) because `_list_wallpapers()` ran two independently-sorted finds and concatenated them; one sort over both streams groups them with their theme. The two PASSES stay separate (D-01/D-03 define live by FOLDER, not extension) and D-17's deliberate end-grouping in the fzf TUI is untouched — that surface is a flat text list where a trailing group plus the marker IS the affordance; the carousel badges every tile. Membership proven unchanged, so --set validation is unaffected. SEPARATE HOST GAP FOUND BY THE SAME MEASUREMENT: Qt on this machine CANNOT DECODE WEBP AT ALL (supportedImageFormats has no webp, canRead false, only gif/ico/jpeg/svg plugins present), so a .webp live wallpaper shows its poster for ever and a .webp still cannot render in the shell. Recorded, NOT 'fixed' — Arch's qt6-imageformats advertises only TIFF/MNG/TGA/WBMP, so it is named a CANDIDATE to verify, not added to install.sh on an unverified claim. Operator asked to copy "Caelestia's file explorer", and I first scoped a QML port of components/filedialog/. WRONG: Caelestia has NO file manager — its file explorer IS Thunar, the same one already installed here. dots README.md:197 `| Super + E | File explorer (default: thunar) |`, hypr/variables.lua:12 `fileExplorer = "thunar"`, manifest.toml:72 installs the thunar package. The look is a ~200-line GTK stylesheet their CLI templates with scheme colours (theme.py:307) plus Papirus folders recoloured to primary. I only found this because the operator pushed back on my "there is no file manager" claim; my first search was filename-based in the SHELL repo and the answer was in the DOTS repo. SHIPPED: thunar.css as a matugen template imported by one line in the stowed gtk-3.0/gtk.css (which already had a slot for exactly this), registered in the contract as css-literal; plus accent-tracked Papirus folders. TWO THINGS WERE ALREADY BUILT — theme_engine_apply_icon_theme and theme_engine_nearest_papirus_color (D-17) existed and were wired in, dormant only because papirus-folders was absent and the icon-theme state said Adwaita (which the function early-returns on). I nearly rebuilt them from scratch. THREE DELIBERATE DIVERGENCES, each forced by measurement not taste: (1) their surfaceContainerLow DOES NOT EXIST on the static-preset branch — none of the 20 palette JSONs define it and matugen FAILS THE WHOLE RENDER rather than degrading, so a wallpaper-only test would have passed while every static theme broke; substituted surface_variant, present in all 20 and already this repo's card/sidebar choice. (2) their `sudo -n papirus-folders` needs root — its own verify_privileges() re-execs under sudo unless the theme is in $HOME, meaning root per theme switch or a 19MB copy that goes stale; replaced with a user-level shadow theme of symlinks under the SAME theme name, exploiting two measured facts (XDG searches ~/.local/share/icons FIRST, and same-named theme dirs MERGE), so only folders are overridden, nothing is copied, and Papirus updates are picked up free — papirus-folders REMOVED from install.sh entirely. (3) GTK3 only, since Thunar is GTK3 and their gtk-4.0 copy can never match. LATENT BUG FIXED: the hue mapper emitted 3 of 23 names that do not exist in Papirus — carmine-red (real name carmine), bright-orange, oxidgreen — so those hue buckets silently recoloured nothing; validated the emitted set against the 109 real colours, mismatch now empty. GUARD: a prefix glob for `blue` also matches folder-bluegrey-docker.svg and would emit a bogus foldergrey-*.svg; suffix must be ".svg" or start with "-", verified 0 malformed across 938 links. VERIFIED LIVE AND I GOT IT WRONG TWICE FIRST. The load-bearing assumption was whether `.thunar` is a real style class. Probe 1 used CSS min-width — useless, Hyprland tiles and overrides client geometry. Probe 2 read ZERO magenta pixels and I nearly concluded the sheet was inert: grim captures a SCREEN REGION, Thunar was behind the terminal, and I measured the terminal — a negative with no positive control. Cause re-read from /usr/bin/xdg-open rather than restated: search_desktop_file does `command="$(get_key Exec | first_word)"` then `env "$command" "$@"` — the first Exec word is run directly, PATH-resolved, and `Terminal` is NEVER consulted. Fix is three parts: a /usr/local/bin/open-in-terminal launcher that reads `.apps.terminal` from prefs.json LIVE on every launch and execs `$term -e "$@"`, two Terminal=false wrapper .desktop entries routing through it (yazi %f, nvim %F — field codes and nvim's MimeType list kept verbatim, not normalised), and AppsPage dropping Terminal=true entries outright instead of labelling a trap the user can still select. WHY /usr/local/bin AND NOT $HOME: a .desktop Exec= cannot contain ~ or $HOME, and the existing precedent (claude-code-url-handler.desktop) hardcodes /home/aorus, which breaks for any other username — an absolute system path is username-independent, and install.sh already had the sudo install -Dm755 pattern to mirror. WHY LIVE-READ AND NOT A GENERATED WRAPPER: a generated `Exec=kitty -e yazi %f` snapshots the terminal and needs a regeneration hook on the apps.terminal row to avoid going stale; reading prefs at launch has neither problem. TWO FINDINGS RESHAPED IT MID-TASK. (1) ~/.local/share/applications is a WHOLE-DIRECTORY STOW FOLD into vscodium/.local/share/applications, so the wrappers went into that existing package — a second package on the same path would force stow to unfold it — and that directory has a deliberate deny-all + explicit-opt-in ignore rule, so both needed `!` lines beside codium.desktop. (2) THERE WAS NO mimeinfo.cache, and this was LOAD-BEARING, not hygiene: without it `gio mime` reports only system entries — measured, it listed yazi.desktop but NOT the stowed codium.desktop despite codium declaring inode/directory — and the pickers build their candidates from exactly that output, so the wrappers would have been invisible. stow.sh now runs update-desktop-database. A SECOND visibility gate: TryExec hides the wrappers until the launcher is installed, so the install.sh step is required, not optional. DesktopEntry.runInTerminal verified real at quickshell-core.qmltypes:386 BEFORE being relied on. Script verified across all five resolution paths against the real binary: configured terminal honoured; apps:null (this host's actual shape), missing prefs.json and malformed JSON each fall back to kitty; a configured-but-UNINSTALLED terminal also falls back rather than failing silently, which would look identical to the bug being fixed; no args gives usage + exit 2. PICKER MODELS COMPUTED, NOT INFERRED FROM THE RENDER (a plausible fallback masked an empty model in this same file two tasks ago): File explorer -> [Yazi File Manager, Thunar File Manager], File editor -> [VSCodium, Neovim], both non-empty, each wrapper replacing its console twin one-for-one. Media playback and File manager were honest InfoRows saying nothing could be set, on the reasoning that nothing in THIS shell launches them - sound for a shell-internal setting, but "Default applications" means the DESKTOP-WIDE default, which is what xdg-open consults. Both are SelectRows now writing `xdg-mime default`. GIO AND XDG-MIME DISAGREE and the planner settled which governs BY READING `/usr/bin/xdg-open` rather than guessing: `XDG_CURRENT_DESKTOP=Hyprland` matches no `detectDE` case, so DE=generic (line 478) routes to `open_generic_xdg_mime`, which calls `xdg-mime query default` - gio's `kitty-open.desktop` answer is display-unsafe here. `yazi.desktop` is `Terminal=true` and xdg-open's generic path IGNORES that (it execs Exec's first word directly), so it is offered with a derived "(needs a terminal)" suffix rather than silently dropped or silently broken - OPEN ITEM, it will not open a window as-is. | 260826-1n9 | SEVEN OPERATOR-REPORTED SETTINGS DEFECTS, and THREE OF THEM WERE ONE BUG. Operator listed "About repeats System information for every info", "Updates uses the ! glyph a lot" and "Apps uses the ! glyph a lot" as three complaints; all three were `common/InfoRow.qml:65` hardcoding `text: "info"` on a type that HAD NO `icon` PROPERTY AT ALL, so every InfoRow on every page rendered the same circled-i. THE FONT WAS NEVER THE PROBLEM — checked the ligature table of `MaterialSymbolsRounded[FILL,GRAD,opsz,wght].ttf` directly: info/update/apps/list/terminal/volume_up/folder/play_circle/download/priority_high/error ALL PRESENT. InfoRow is pre-existing (260821-6z1) so `icon` defaults to "info" and no existing consumer changes. THE OPERATOR THEN SAID "3 and 5 also have separate bugs besides the ! glyph" AND WAS RIGHT ON BOTH. Updates carried two more: `label: "Pending update"` static on every package row (the identical bug class as About\'s `label: "System information"` at AboutPage.qml:121, and BOTH had been justified in-plan as "RowIndex\'s jump key is an exact label match" — a real constraint used to rationalise an unreadable UI), and the raw `checkupdates`/`paru -Qua` output pushed into subtext COMPLETELY UNPARSED, so the requested grid had no fields to populate. Apps carried A RUNTIME TYPE MISMATCH THAT SILENTLY BROKE THREE CONSUMERS: `AppsPage.qml:50` wrote `value: e.id` (a desktop-entry id) into `apps.terminal`/`apps.audio`, whose Prefs defaults are "kitty"/"pavucontrol" (EXECUTABLES) and whose three consumers exec them directly — `SystemCapsule.qml:588` and `UpdatesPage.qml:117` as `[terminal,"-e",...]`, and `SessionPage.qml:194` with `--class` and `-o background_opacity=0.85`, KITTY-ONLY FLAGS. Picking anything from that picker broke the update action and the idle-overrides editor with no error. The picker also modelled ALL ~52 desktop entries, which is why the operator called it "unrelated"; now Category-filtered (TerminalEmulator; Audio/AudioVideo/Mixer) and storing something executable. SEARCH-JUMP WAS FOUR DEFECTS ON ONE PATH, all found by reading, none by guessing: (1) `goToPage` ends in `currentPageIdx = idx` and QML EMITS NO SIGNAL WHEN THE VALUE IS UNCHANGED, so a same-page result — the common case, the window opens on page 0 — fired nothing at all AND left `pendingRowLabel` set to poison the next real swap; (2) `_recollectRows()` runs in the statement right after synchronous incubation, before Qt\'s polish pass positions the Column and sizes the Flickable, so every row mapped to y approximately 0 and `contentY` never moved — THE RIGHT ROW WAS RINGED, RINGED OFF-SCREEN; (3) `_collectFocusableRows` recursed `children` with no visibility test and QQC2 StackView keeps non-current pages parented at `visible:false`, so it could ring a row on the invisible page underneath (this was the PRIOR task\'s unverified checklist item 2); (4) `_swapTo` called the StackView\'s own `openSubPage` directly, never touching `subPageIdxStack`, so depth was 2 while the stack was empty and Escape closed the WINDOW instead of popping — and the premature recollect cleared the pending label before the deferred pass could use it. FROST: `Settings.qml:128` was `Colours.surface`, fully opaque; it is an XDG TOPLEVEL (class org.quickshell, windowrules.lua:65) NOT a layer surface, so blur comes from global `decoration:blur` (size 8, passes 3) acting on a translucent window — no layer_rule. Operator chose 0.78 (`SectionPopout.qml:1038` panelSurfaceOpacity) over 0.38 (`Launcher.qml:188`, mirroring Dashboard). WEATHER CITY REVERSES 260825-wj2\'s READ-ONLY DECISION on operator instruction, and it was BIGGER THAN THE BRIEF IMPLIED: `WeatherBackend.qml:418` builds its query from lat/lon ALONE and `cityOverride` only suppresses the reverse geocode and relabels the readout, so a typed city needed a FORWARD geocode or the feature would have looked like it worked and changed nothing — routed as a second path on GeocodeBackend\'s EXISTING nominatim host, no new host. THE GATE THAT SHOULD HAVE EXISTED ALL ALONG: Task 1 shipped `qml-import-check` FIRST AND ALONE, proven clean over the untouched tree before any QML edit, because the prior task shipped a shell that would not start after a reboot (`common/StackPage.qml` missing `import ".."`) while settings-index-check 167/0, colour-lint 398/0, motion-lint 585/0 AND qmllint were all green — QMLLINT RETURNS 0 ON A MISSING QML IMPORT. Orchestrator falsified the new gate against that EXACT historical regression rather than trusting the executor: removing the import line gives "1 unresolved reference" and EXIT 1, restoring gives 0 and exit 0, while qmllint returns 0 BOTH WAYS. Gates re-run independently by the orchestrator with real (unpiped) exit codes: qml-import-check 0 unresolved/135 files, settings-index 176/0, colour-lint 401/0, motion-lint 588/0. TWO VERIFY-COMMAND IMPRECISIONS MEASURED AND REPORTED RATHER THAN GLOSSED: the GeocodeBackend URL-uniqueness grep counts 2 because the userAgent\'s github.com contact URL matches the endpoint regex (pre-existing, confirmed against the pre-Task-7 commit; github.com is never an XHR target), and Task 5\'s `\\-\\>` regex CAN NEVER MATCH literal "->" because `\\>` is a word-boundary anchor in GNU grep -E, not a literal — it gated nothing, but it proved nothing either. | 260825-wj2 | THE FOUR CAELESTIA SETTINGS-PAGE GROUPS, plus the sub-page mechanism they turned out to require. Scoped in `.planning/todos/pending/2026-08-25-caelestia-settings-pages.md`, which said "too big for /gsd-quick"; OPERATOR EXPLICITLY OVERRODE THAT ("we did larger work as quick tasks and they were fine") and it shipped in six atomic commits. THE TODO WAS WRONG ABOUT THE SCOPE IN THREE PLACES, all caught by reading the vendored Caelestia source instead of reconstructing it. (1) PER-APP VOLUME ALREADY SHIPPED IN FULL — slider, empty state, full-mixer relay; the todo's "the PipeWire half is done" understated it, the UI half was done too, and `AudioBackend.setStreamMuted` already existed. The real gap was ONE MUTE TOGGLE, so that group collapsed from a feature to a row. (2) CAELESTIA'S APPS PAGE IS NOT A FLAT PAGE and the planner's first design (a tri-state Normal/Favourite/Hidden SelectRow per app, ~52 rows on one scroll) was its own invention. Their real shape is three levels — `AppsPage` (four default-app popup rows + a Library NavRow) -> `apps/AllApps` -> `apps/AppInfo`, where Favourite and Hidden are two plain ToggleRows. Their Bluetooth page drills the same way (`BluetoothPage.qml:153` openSubPage(1) device info, `:176` openSubPage(2) pairing). OUR SETTINGS TREE HAD NO SUB-PAGE MECHANISM AT ALL — `grep -rn 'isSubPage\|openSubPage\|subPage' modules/settings/` returned nothing. Operator was shown both shapes as ASCII previews and chose to BUILD THE MECHANISM and follow the reference for Apps and Bluetooth both, over the cheaper flat page. (3) THEIR DEFAULT-APP ROWS WRITE SHELL-INTERNAL COMMANDS, NOT xdg-mime, which killed the planner's xdg-mime design; measuring our actual consumers found Terminal has two (`SystemCapsule.qml:581`, `SessionPage.qml:191`), Audio one (`MenuTree.qml:187`), FILE MANAGER NONE (`FilesMode.qml:39` already goes through `xdg-open`) and MEDIA PLAYBACK NONE — so two real controls and two honest InfoRows rather than four controls, two of which would do nothing. THE GATE HAD TO CHANGE NO MATTER WHAT, and that was the finding that most changed the plan: `settings-index-check` maps pageIdx->file by taking the FIRST `*Page` match after each `property Component <name>:` line, so a `StackPage` wrapper makes that first match the literal `StackPage`, the gate looks for `pages/StackPage.qml`, and CHECK A fails "mapped file does not exist" — leaving sub-page rows unindexed would NOT have kept it green. Smallest change that keeps every existing check meaningful: `RowIndex` gains an OPTIONAL `subPageIdx` (absent = 0, so ALL 113 EXISTING ENTRIES STAY BYTE-IDENTICAL and CHECK A's existing grep key still matches), the comp->file parser collects every `*Page` identifier in declaration order skipping `StackPage`, CHECK A/B key on the `(pageIdx, subPageIdx)` pair, NEW CHECK F proves every sub-page component resolves to a file declared in `pages/qmldir`, and search deep-links via `jumpSubPageIdx` because a selection-dependent sub-page cannot be opened cold (App info rows index under subPageIdx 2 but search lands on All apps). STEPPERROW WAS PORTED ON THE REFERENCE-WINS RULE, NOT ASKED ABOUT: Caelestia uses `StepperRow` for every polling value (`ServicesPage.qml:100,111,121`) and reserves `SelectRow` for genuine enumerations (`:137` lyrics source, `:146` preferred player), so substituting SelectRow for a numeric interval changes the INTERACTION, not the widget — and the operator had already taken the match-the-reference side of that exact trade on the sub-page question an hour earlier. ONE DIVERGENCE THE PLANNER PUSHED BACK WITH AND WAS RIGHT ABOUT: their StepperRow wraps their own StyledSpinBox, but `common/SliderRow.qml`'s own header records that the QQC2 `Slider` it used to wrap RENDERED NOTHING AT ALL here — no track, no handle, not even its overridden delegates' explicit-pixel Rectangles — across 13 call sites, and that it was hand-rolled specifically to avoid "a THIRD QQC2 interactive primitive risking a THIRD blind spot". Ours ports the reference's interaction and property set verbatim and hand-rolls the `- value +` pill on the proven ToggleRow/SelectRow idiom. Divergence in IMPLEMENTATION, not convention, backed by a failure this repo already paid for. THE GATE WAS EDITED TWICE and both edits carry a mandatory inline falsification (FAIL->PASS recorded), because adding `StepperRow` to the row-primitive alternation while zero StepperRows exist is a NO-OP for every current count — a green gate would have proved nothing about it. (1) ZEN OPACITY: `windowrules.lua:219` had Zen in the house translucency family at `0.90 0.88`, and 0.90 is enough for the wallpaper to read through a playing video. Focused is 1.0 now, unfocused stays 0.88 — operator decision D-1, with "fully opaque always" and "drop translucency everywhere" both offered and rejected. (2) SUPER+TAB HAD NO EXIT ANIMATION AT ALL, and that is the whole finding — it was never a wrong animation. `Overview.qml` arms and runs `Cascade` on `Component.onCompleted` (:95-98) but NEVER CALLS `Cascade.runExit()`; all five dismissal routes emitted `dismissRequested()` straight at shell.qml, which answered `overviewLoader.active = false` and destroyed the wl_surface on frame one. What looked like a dismiss was Hyprland's own layer-close. THE SAME DEFECT CLASS AS 260825-x9p ROUND 3 AND THE CONFIG PANELS BEFORE IT: the property a loader is keyed on must be cleared by the END of the exit, never by the caller that starts it. | 260819-426 | THE REVERSE GEOCODE NEVER RAN. Operator asked whether the geolocation lookup was accurate after the label still read 'Cairo' in Alexandria — it was accurate, it was simply never called. `_revalidateAgainstSettings()` is reached during construction (state FileView load -> loadCache() -> _revalidateAgainstSettings()) and called `_resolveCityIfNeeded()`, which together with the GeocodeBackend child was declared ~200 lines LOWER in the file and did not yet exist on the object: `TypeError: Property '_resolveCityIfNeeded' ... is not a function`, 18 occurrences in ~/.cache/quickshell.log. Every call threw, `city` stayed empty, and the chain fell through to the timezone fallback — which prints 'Cairo' for Africa/Cairo whether the coordinates are Cairo's OR Alexandria's, so the bug produced a PLAUSIBLE WRONG ANSWER rather than a visible failure, and was equally invisible back when the coordinates really were Cairo's. Four hypotheses eliminated by measurement before the fix: Nominatim returns 200+Alexandria for the component's exact URL and UA (403 with no UA, so the header matters and IS sent); Qt puts the custom User-Agent on the wire verbatim (proven against a local listener — the plan-time check only proved `user-agent` is absent from Qt's forbidden-header table, which was necessary but NOT sufficient); Qt reaches Nominatim over TLS (qml6 harness, HTTP 200); wiring and brace depth both correct. Fix: both declarations moved above the call site with a comment marking the order load-bearing. 18 TypeErrors before reload, 0 after. Also recorded: the systemd unit journals NOTHING — every QML warning goes to ~/.cache/quickshell.log, the launcher's sink — and console.log is invisible under qml6, so the throwaway harness had to report over a local socket Then the symptom PERSISTED, revealing a SECOND independent silent fault: `_resolveCityIfNeeded()` runs from the parent's onDrawerOpenChanged, and at that instant the child's `drawerOpen: root.drawerOpen` binding has NOT been re-evaluated — instrumentation caught parent=true / child=false in the same tick — so resolve() returned early at GeocodeBackend's own guard. Seven guard clauses across the two functions, NONE of which logs on early return, which is why total failure produced a plausible label and no error. Fixed with Qt.callLater(geocoder.resolve) to defer past binding settlement, keeping every guard intact rather than deleting the child's. Verified live: 'Geocode parsed city=Alexandria status=200', cityLabelSource=geocoded, cache city='Alexandria'. Method note: `hyprctl dispatch global quickshell:dashboard` FAILS under the Lua config (hyprctl wraps args in hl.dispatch()); the working form is `hyprctl dispatch 'hl.dsp.global("quickshell:dashboard")'` | 2026-08-19 | 2853d13, dcf6f35 | [260819-426-fix-the-geocode-typeerror-declare-the-re](./quick/260819-426-fix-the-geocode-typeerror-declare-the-re/) |

The centre was a single pane; it now carries a two-tab bar INSIDE its existing 48px header band (TabBar one-way-synced FROM a SwipeView pager, the Dashboard idiom) so not one pixel of the Wayland layer surface moved — proven by a git-diff geometry grep that must print 0, pinned to the `notifSurfaceWidth` token rather than a bare `implicitWidth:` which would have false-positived on countCapsule's deleted self-sizing lines. The notification count folded into the tab label. XmlListModel was MEASURED AND REJECTED before planning: Qt6 dropped its `xml` string property and it has no `get()` (only reload/errorString), so it can drive a view delegate and nothing else — filtering, merging and caching from JS are impossible, which would have made the repo's WeatherBackend cache idiom unbuildable. Architecture is XHR + `responseXML` + a manual `childNodes` walk; `getElementsByTagName` is ABSENT from Qt's QML DOM and was the real thrower behind an early misread. One ~20-line walker covers RSS and Atom, CDATA via concatenated child nodeValues, Atom href via attributes[]. Sources live in a seeded, hand-editable news-sources.json (BBC World, NPR, LWN) — both it and news-cache.json registered in contract.json engine_owned_files, and that task was sequenced FIRST because theme-doctor reddens on any unaccounted state entry and lib/commit.sh builds rsync --delete excludes from the same array, so an unregistered cache would have been deleted by the next theme-apply. The one-host-per-fenced-file rule was DELIBERATELY WIDENED and recorded in the file header: feed hosts live in operator-editable JSON by design, so the literal-host fence was replaced by an https:// allowlist enforced at two points (source validation and item acceptance) — the second is what lets the pane call Qt.openUrlExternally with no confirm dialog, unlike NotifCard.qml:587. Dropdown uses the inline-expand idiom, NOT a QQC2 Popup: zero ComboBox/Popup/Menu exist anywhere in the tree, so a QQC2 popup inside a layer surface is unproven on this stack. Refresh is open-gated on NotifServer.centreOpen with stale-while-revalidate (~15min TTL) and a manual button — zero timers and zero network while closed. | 260819-m94 | News tab card view, left thumbnails and tab glyphs — the three visual changes the operator asked for on top of 260819-6oy, plus a source-list change that fell out of MEASURING them rather than being requested. Design settled in an approved spec first (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-19-news-tab-cards-design.md, b384595); every number in it was measured on this host, none assumed. THUMBNAIL COVERAGE WAS THE HINGE: of the shipped 3 sources only BBC ships per-item images (15/40 items); LWN ships none; and NPR's exist ONLY inside content:encoded HTML where the FIRST <img> of every item is a tracking pixel (npr-rss-pixel.png?story=...) — rendering it would have sent a story-identifying request to NPR analytics on every scroll. NPR was therefore DROPPED, not scraped. Replaced by Ars Technica after probing 14 candidate feeds: it is the only one shipping a purpose-built 500x500 SQUARE media:thumbnail (64KB) — exactly what a square slot wants, no cropping — against 978KB (Tom's Hardware) and 850KB (Engadget) for an identical 76px result, i.e. ~14MB vs ~1MB per 15-item refresh. Phoronix added text-only on domain fit: its feed has literally zero images across all three endpoints (verified: no media:*, no enclosure, no <img> in 21,729 bytes), and reaching the og:image on its article pages would cost 82KB/0.87s PER PAGE (~1.2MB, ~13s per refresh) plus browser-UA spoofing, since Phoronix 403s non-browser clients — explicitly refused. It renders the glyph fallback, as LWN already does. LAYOUT: left thumbnail, mirroring NotifCard.qml's iconSlot and inheriting its hard rule verbatim (slot always reserved, never blank, card never changes width, tiered fallback ending in a Material Symbols glyph). Sized 76px because row height is CONTENT-driven — the text column is already ~70px, so 76 costs 6px/row over NotifCard's 42, not 34. Reddit's full-width card-view hero was rejected on measurement: BBC ships 240px into a 430px pane = 1.66x upscale. (Recorded but NOT taken: the BBC CDN honours a width rewrite, /standard/480/ -> 480x270 through /1024/ -> 1024x576, all 200 — unnecessary since 240 is already 3x oversampled for a 76px slot.) GLYPHS are ligatures in Material Symbols Rounded, so a wrong name renders as LITERAL TEXT, not a fallback glyph — every name was verified by HarfBuzz shaping and the gate POISON-TESTED: 'notification' (singular) shapes to 576px of words vs 48px for a real glyph. Toggle persists as view_mode in news-sources.json via a surgical parse->set-one-key->write, NOT writeAdapter(), which would re-serialise `sources` — recorded at NewsBackend.qml:342-353 as not a native JS Array on this Qt build — and risk clobbering the operator's hand-edited source list. PLANNER CAUGHT A LIVE DEFECT: the predecessor's QQC2 fence gate is ALREADY self-falsified in shipped code — NewsPane.qml:173 contains the word Popup inside a comment saying it does not use one, so the gate reads 1 against clean code (verified independently: 1 raw, 0 comment-stripped). Every banned-identifier gate now strips // lines before counting. Three executor traps flagged and avoided: `visible: false` does NOT stop a Qt Image loading (only the `source` binding does — the entire zero-request-in-compact promise rests on this); re-anchoring the text column to the thumb shifts compact by 16px unless leftMargin is mode-conditional; and NewsBackend.qml's header named the dropped source in two places. ALL OPERATOR-VERIFIED 2026-08-19 — Ars thumbnails, the glyph fallback, the toggle and its restart-persistence all confirmed live (persistence proved incidentally: the seed still ships view_mode=compact while the host file records the operator's 'cards'). A TEN-COMMIT follow-up wave then landed against operator reports, and the last of them is the one worth carrying forward. (1) LWN showed 3 headlines instead of 15 — NOT a cache or refresh bug, both were working: _finishRun() sorted all candidates globally by date and truncated to max_items_total with no per-source floor, so the slowest publisher was evicted by faster neighbours. Invisible with 3 sources because the candidate count happened to EQUAL the cap (15+15+10=40, nothing cut); a 4th source made it 57->40 by pure recency. Now round-robin by source, then sorted for display; simulated against real counts (10/10/10/10, old 3-source distribution unchanged, starved source absorbed by others, no loss under the cap). (2) Partial-run failures silently DELETED a whole source's headlines — only the zero-successful case was guarded, and measurement found 4 network-error events across 24 runs, i.e. the partial case is the common one; failure branches now record source NAMES and _finishRun carries those items forward. (3) Reopening the centre on News showed the News tab with Notifications content: the surface is never destroyed, only hidden, and pagerList's StrictlyEnforceRange makes a ListView WRITE its own currentIndex when contentX resets to 0 on re-show — an imperative write that destroys the binding. Dashboard.qml is immune only because it is RECREATED per open, so there was no prior art to copy. (4) The dropdown pushed the list down (it was selectorHost's second Column child); now an anchored sibling on a raised z, with its own opaque backing since notifSurface is 0.38 alpha. (5) motion-lint's QML raw-value check was anchored on a lowercase `duration:` and is case-sensitive, so EVERY camelCased duration property went untested — NotifCentre's own pager documented the hole in a comment rather than the lint closing it; widened and poison-tested, 0 new hits repo-wide. (6) THE METHOD LESSON: the tab pill took FOUR attempts because three were reasoned from source and all three were wrong, each widening the defect. Arithmetic 'proved' the tabs fit (338px content in 366px available) by measuring what they WANTED, never what the container GAVE them. One screenshot settled it in seconds: TabBar DIVIDES ITS WIDTH EQUALLY and ignores implicitWidth, so both pills rendered 183px = 366/2 while tab 0 needed 201 — the tabs OVERLAPPED and tab 1's capsule painted over tab 0's count. Fixed with `width: implicitWidth`. Also corrected a standing note that had caused the avoidance: `grim -g` against one layer surface's hyprctl geometry is SAFE here (ran twice, compositor alive both times) — the known SIGSEGV is the FULLSCREEN capture path, and a blanket rule had been generalised from one incident. Adding a source PROBES the pasted URL live before anything is written, and prefills an EDITABLE name field from the feed document's own channel/feed title. SCOPE HISTORY WORTH KEEPING: the seed estimated itself PHASE-sized and the operator chose quick-task execution anyway; the orchestrator's first scoping cut live-probing and editable names, and the operator restored BOTH mid-flight, which is what pushed the plan to 4 tasks instead of quick mode's 1-3 — the planner said so plainly rather than cramming or silently dropping. Only drag-to-reorder stayed cut (headlines sort by date and selection is round-robin by source, so list order affects tie-breaks only), plus per-source tunables (max_items_per_source, ttl_minutes) which stay hand-edited. FOUR MEASURED FINDINGS DROVE THE DESIGN. (1) `sources` CANNOT drive an editor: it is a readonly computed property projecting to {name,url} and dropping every enabled:false entry, so a separate `allSources` accessor was required. (2) `selectedSource` is compared BY NAME, not URL (NewsBackend.qml:167-169; NewsPane.qml:178/383/412/727) and fetched items carry `source: sourceName` — so a rename must move the filter AND the already-fetched headlines, and a delete/disable must reset the filter and prune items, or the dropdown and the list silently disagree until the next refresh. All mutation functions are therefore keyed by URL, never by index, so a shifting list cannot mis-target a row. (3) Two name-rendering Text elements had NO width cap and NO elide, and a character-count bound cannot fix that because Design.qml:98-107 deliberately pins no font family — the shell inherits the GTK font, so a 32-char name has no knowable pixel width. Data is bounded at the input (32 chars) and LAYOUT is bounded in pixels, separately. Markup was already inert: all five name-rendering Texts are PlainText. (4) NotifCentre.qml:220 is WlrKeyboardFocus.OnDemand, so a TextField in this layer surface can actually receive keystrokes, with WifiPanel.qml:993-1015 as the shipped precedent including Escape consumed locally so it never reaches the centre-closing handler. ARCHITECTURE: `_fetchXml(url, onDone)` extracted as the ONE transport — exactly one XMLHttpRequest construction survives in the file (verified independently). probeSource() is a second CALLER, not a second fetcher: it keeps its own probeState register and — proven by an awk-scoped grep over just its function body — never touches requestsInFlight, sourcesOk, sourcesFailed, _runBuffer or lastError, so an in-flight refresh cannot be corrupted by a probe or vice versa. It registers in _activeXhrs, so closing the centre aborts it, and it carries the same D-32 open-gate refresh() does — zero network while the centre is closed still holds. Commit uses the PROBED url, never the live field text, so a URL edited after a successful probe cannot be committed under the stale probe's title. Every mutation persists through _writeSources(), the one surgical read-modify-write path mirroring setViewMode()'s refuse-on-malformed guard — never a whole-object republish, which would re-serialise a `sources` that is not a native JS Array on this Qt build. TWO JUDGMENT CALLS, both made against existing precedent rather than invented: delete is an inline row-scoped two-step confirm copying WifiPanel.qml's Forget shape (this repo has no modal precedent at all), and atomicWrites was REFUSED on sourcesFile because whether watchChanges still fires across an atomic replace is unmeasured on this build and the entire editor depends on that watch — recovery is cheap (the write path refuses to compound a torn file; stow.sh:382 re-seeds on absence). ONE DEVIATION: _reasonText() moved from Task 4 into Task 3, because Task 3's rename error line cannot render a real reason without it and a bare code like "duplicate-name" would not meet its own done bar; Task 4 reuses it unchanged. The executor also caught TWO self-inflicted gate false-fails during execution — its own explanatory comments twice repeated a gate's literal search token (onSourcesChanged, _fetchXml(), addSource()) — the same self-matching-comment defect the predecessor task first found, now hit from the authoring side rather than the reading side. | 28 | 260819-ug4 — News tab self-healing retry. refresh(force)'s non-forced path gated on WHOLE-CACHE staleness only, so one source failing while its siblings succeeded read as a dead feed for the rest of the ttl_minutes window: the run wrote a cache that was nominally fresh but missing that source, and every later centre-open logged "refresh() skipped — cache fresh, N minute(s) old". OBSERVED END TO END before the fix: Hacker News took one transient network error (status 0) at 21:02, 40 items were written from the other three sources, and HN read as dead until the TTL rolled over, at which point it fetched 10 items normally — the feed was never broken. THE RETRY SET IS DERIVED FROM CACHE CONTENTS, not from _failedNames: a configured enabled source with zero items in the current cache is exactly a source that failed or returned nothing, which is restart-durable (_failedNames is in-memory and returns [] after a restart) and additionally catches a source that parses cleanly but yields zero items — that increments sourcesOk, never enters _failedNames, and is indistinguishable to the operator from a dead feed. Accepted cost, named rather than hidden: a feed that legitimately publishes nothing is re-attempted each centre-open — one bounded idempotent GET behind the D-32 gate on a list hard-capped at maxSources 8; and if an operator sets max_items_total below the source count, round-robin starves the tail sources of slots so they retry every open, bounded and logged. PARTIAL-RUN MERGE, the most likely way to get this wrong: new _okNames (positive twin of _failedNames) drives both the publish gate and _carryForward(), which carries an item when its source IS currently configured AND is NOT in _okNames — byte-identical to the old _failedNames keying on a whole run, and on a partial run it also carries the never-attempted sources, which is what stops the healthy sources being clobbered. The "currently configured" half preserves the existing behaviour that a source hand-removed from news-sources.json has its items dropped. Publish gate keys on _okNames.length > 0 rather than the counter because sourcesOk is now SEEDED with the deliberately-skipped healthy count (sources.length - targets.length) to keep NewsPane.qml:1123's "N of M sources unreachable" counting M over all configured sources; a counter-only gate would have let a wholly-failed partial run take the publish branch. A partial run does NOT advance fetchedAtMs — that would lie in the age readout and push the TTL out, indefinitely deferring the full refresh the healthy sources still need — but it does still writeCache(), so a heal survives a restart. NOT TOUCHED: NotifCentre.qml:721's newsRefreshButton, which already exists and whose refresh(true) already bypasses the stale gate. AN EARLIER CLAIM IN THIS FILE THAT NewsPane.qml SHIPPED NO MANUAL REFRESH WAS WRONG AND IS RETRACTED (361d99e) — it came from grepping one file when the control lives in the parent component; the operator caught it by asking "don't we already have a manual refresh?", which shrank this task from two halves to one. Gates: qmllint exit 0, new comment-stripped Python gate news-selfheal-gate 5/5 (falsified by hand before being trusted), colour-lint 150/0, motion-lint 297/0, quickshell-doctor 28/0 on re-run (a first run read 27/1, the known post-hot-reload flake). The no-op path logged one 'every source has headlines — nothing to re-attempt' line with zero fetches. Exercise A treated as SUBSUMED by C (same three assertions; it differs only in creating the zero-item state via a watched-file rename instead of a doctored cache plus restart). NOT exercised: the all-attempted-sources-fail partial run, which needs a deliberately unreachable host. BOUNDARY FOUND DURING VERIFICATION from the operator's log rather than by reasoning: a source that FAILS but still holds previously-cached items is NOT re-attempted, because _carryForward() preserves those items and _sourcesNeedingRetry() keys on zero cached items — observed at log lines 8508 -> 8531 -> 8532, where Hacker News failed, kept its 10 carried items, and the next open said 'every source has headlines'. Coherent with the defect this task targeted (a feed reading as DEAD, i.e. blank) but narrower than 'retry anything that failed': a stale-but-populated source still waits out the TTL. M-2: the stow pre-create protects nothing on its own — tpm 3.1.0 DEFAULT_TPM_PATH is $HOME/.tmux/plugins/ and it only computes an XDG path when TMUX_PLUGIN_MANAGER_PATH is unset, so set-environment -g at tmux.conf:26 must precede the run line or the pre-created dir is simply unused. M-10 INCIDENTAL, pre-existing: tmux itself was never declared in PACMAN_PKGS — a fresh install had no multiplexer at all, quite apart from theming — now added. | 260823-9ak | Edge bar — always-on top/bottom screen-edge strips the dashboard and launcher attach to and animate from, with a settings toggle (default ON), a static centre bulge, palette-driven colour, and smooth concave attachment corners in BOTH modes. AWAITING OPERATOR SIGN-OFF: Task 7 is a blocking human-verify checkpoint and was deliberately NOT self-certified — the hover-dwell interaction cannot be exercised here (no input-injection tool on this host, and wtype types into the focused window). SETTLED BY THE OPERATOR BEFORE ANY CODE: 'app drawer' means the DASHBOARD, not a new app grid; the edge bar is an INDEPENDENT FRAME (two new surfaces, Bar.qml untouched, verified in zero commits) because the live bar orientation is `vertical`/right-edge so a top+bottom edge bar definitively is not the bar; and the extra thickness is a STATIC permanent bulge, a discoverable landmark, not a hover-reactive swell. THE ROLE-CHOICE BUG WORTH REMEMBERING: the strip first shipped filled with `Colours.surface` and the operator reported it 'completely black and not color shifting'. The binding was LIVE and the per-theme values DID differ — `surface` is simply the PAGE BACKGROUND role and is a near-black on all 16 dark palettes (gruvbox #282828, catppuccin #1e1e2e, tokyonight #1a1b26, vantablack #000000), so at 0.55 alpha over a dark wallpaper it read as flat black. Wrong role, not a broken binding, and the magenta fallback never firing was the proof. The shell's real colour-shifting edge language is GradientBorder.qml's primary->secondary->tertiary accent stops, used by 13 surfaces. GradientBorder itself CANNOT be mounted on this strip: it builds its ring from `_roundedRect()`, so on a bulged outline it would trace the bounding box and drop the bulge — the idiom was transplanted onto EdgeBar's own `_outlinePath` instead. One deliberate divergence: GradientBorder sweeps endpoints around a half-diagonal, which on a ~3440x16 strip compresses the spectrum into 16px at 90 degrees and pulses, so the axis is LOCKED to the long axis and scrolled, seamless via RepeatSpread with the stop list closing back on primary. GATES ARE BLIND TO THIS WHOLE CLASS: doctor 28/0, colour-lint 359/0 and motion-lint 546/0 were green while the strip rendered black, and stayed green after the fix — the only channel that showed anything was quickshell.log, and even there the one warning seen was a STALE intermediate-edit artifact that two consecutive forced reloads cleared. THREE MEASURED FINDINGS FROM PLANNING: quickshell-doctor's surface registry is a closed contract whose `bar/SectionPopout.qml\|quickshell-bar-\|pattern` row matches ANY `quickshell-bar-*` namespace and the live half demands exactly one row match, so the strips are namespaced `quickshell-baredge-` (no hyphen after `bar`) — the identical workaround already documented for `quickshell-bardrawer-`/`quickshell-bartip-`. A second RESERVING surface is legal because the doctor's `exclusiveZone: 0` assertion applies only to rows marked `noreserve`. And req 7's flare had to be an ADDITIVE sibling component: both panels' fills are plain `Rectangle`s that cannot render a concave corner at all, so editing GradientBorder alone would have flared the rim and left the fill square — a fix that passes every gate and looks broken. FILE PLACEMENT IS LOAD-BEARING: EdgeBar.qml lives at `modules/`, NOT `modules/bar/`, because the `bar-colour-role-routing` check scans `modules/bar/` and would force the strip through BarRoles instead of reading `Colours.*` directly as R9 needs. EXECUTOR DEVIATION: moved Dashboard.qml's `clip: true` from `panel` to `content` — `panel`'s clip would have silently hidden the Task 2 flares, which must paint outside its bounds. Reservation proof `[0,8,50,8]` ON / `[0,0,50,0]` OFF, byte-identical to pre-Task-3. ### Pending Todos

[From .planning/todos/pending/ — ideas captured during sessions]

**`.planning/todos/pending/` is EMPTY as of 2026-08-26.** The `retirement-check`
matches-own-prose todo was closed by quick task 260826-npc (`882fea85`, `13a0cb89`)
and moved to `todos/completed/`. The `steamwebhelper` entry below is kept here for
its root-cause finding only — **its file already lives in `todos/completed/`**, since
the fix is not available from this repo. It is not queued work.

1. **`steamwebhelper` crash loop — reason 213 = `INVALID_INITIATOR_ORIGIN`** (`2026-08-23-steamwebhelper-crash-loop-on-nvidia-xwayland.md`, severity `minor`) — **ROOT CAUSE NAMED 2026-08-25.** Read Chromium's own `content/browser/bad_message.h` at tag `126.0.6478.126` (this build is `.183`, same branch): enum index 213 is `INVALID_INITIATOR_ORIGIN`, between `RFPH_POST_MESSAGE_INVALID_SOURCE_ORIGIN` (212) and `RFHI_BEGIN_NAVIGATION_MISSING_INITIATOR_ORIGIN` (214). CEF 126.2's own `CefFrame::LoadRequest` docs corroborate verbatim. **It is a navigation-security check with ZERO graphics involvement** — which explains why `-cef-disable-gpu` and the `htmlcache` wipe both did nothing (category errors, not deep problems), and retires the "NVIDIA + XWayland" framing along with the "test a different NVIDIA driver branch" angle. **The timing was also re-measured and the original characterisation was WRONG:** all 12/12 kills fire 3-5s after a webhelper browser start, not "minutes to hours apart" — that gap was measuring when the *user* next started the webhelper. So the old "judge any fix over hours" rule is retired in favour of a restart-count protocol (start N times, count kills within 5s), which is both cheaper and stronger. Not every start dies (40 starts / 12 kills), so it is startup-bound but not deterministic. **Not fixable from this repo** — it is Steam's own code. `--disable-site-isolation-trials` would suppress it but is NOT recommended: a real security downgrade in the process rendering the logged-in Steam store, traded against a cosmetic annoyance. No upstream Valve issue names 213; filing one is the highest-value remaining action. Workaround unchanged: `steam -shutdown`.

## Session Continuity

SHIPPED 2026-08-27 — **260827-ar3: hyprlock retired, repo and host. Task 8 of 260827-833 closed; NOTHING IS OWED.** `7578f95c`, package removed by the operator.

**THE GATE, NOT THE BRIEF, IS THE SOURCE OF THE TOUCHPOINT LIST.** Seven per the original brief, ten per 260827-833's SUMMARY, **25 files** per a tree-wide grep. All three are answers to the wrong question. `retirement-check` has 16 scan classes with fixed targets; the actionable set is "files a blocking class greps", which is far smaller than "files that mention it" and is *derivable in one read of the gate*. Deriving it turned a guess into arithmetic and found the two traps below.

**ONLY ONE SCAN CLASS SKIPS COMMENTS — AND MY OWN CHANGELOG NOTE WALKED INTO IT.** `grep_hits(..., skip_comment_lines=True)` is passed by `scan_cross_package_refs` and no other scanner; the gate's own comment says so ("The ONE caller that skips whole-line comments"). So prose is FATAL in `keybinds`, `contract-json`, `matugen-templates`, `test-fixtures`, `install-stow-lists` and harmless everywhere else. I wrote "hyprlock REMOVED 2026-08-27" into `install.sh` — class 10, the exact gate this task exists to turn green. Caught on the post-edit grep, not by reasoning. **After removing a token, grep for the token again.** Second recorded instance of this class.

**THE ONE DANGEROUS CHECK WAS ALREADY ANSWERED, IN CODE, BY THE PREVIOUS AUTHOR.** `/etc/pam.d/hyprlock` is package-owned and dies with `-Rns` — the only way this removal could have locked the operator out. It was safe because `LockPam.qml` names `config: "passwd"`, owned by **shadow 4.20.0.arch1-1**, a choice 260827-833's author made *specifically* to survive this removal and documented in the file header. Verified against `pacman -Qo` on both files rather than trusting the comment (a comment describes intent, not result); both facts held, and the file needed no edit.

**GATES: `retirement-check hyprlock` `failed_classes=0`; `theme-doctor` 1330 passed / 0 failed — the first fully-green doctor run recorded in this file.** Also `theme-parity` 1897/0, `hypr-equivalence-check` PASS 3 FAIL 0, `keybind-doctor` 13/0, quickshell `Configuration Loaded`. `pacman -Rsp` printed exactly one package — no dependency cascade, every dep shared with hyprland.

**SCOPE DISCIPLINE:** corrected only prose the removal made FALSE (README's lock-screen row + two tree listings, four "retirement is HELD" comments, `wallpaper.sh`'s citation of a deleted file, `LockPam.qml`'s note to past tense). Left the 2026-07-28 migration design doc untouched as a point-in-time record — same treatment `v3.0-MILESTONE-AUDIT.md` got — and left accurate historical comments alone. `repo-prose` is report-domain, so this costs nothing.
